For value(i) algorithm in manual form:
function V(i):
 if i==0 return v0.
 for r=i downto 1:
   b=B[r]
   if b is const (in0/in1/in2/qk): c = bit; if c==0: return parity(i,r) [1 if (i-r) even else 0]
   else b=n_j: if V(j)==0: return parity(i,r)
   # else continue
 return v0 if i even else 1-v0.
Where parity(i,r)= 1 if (i-r)%2==0 else 0. Note if r=i (source_zero at i), returns1. Correct: if source bit 0, NAND output=1.
